subscribe(listener: (message: unknown) => void): () => void;\n}\n\nexport type StopHarnessRuntime = () => Promise<void>;\n\n/** The part of a process `ManagedRuntime` that runs the harness's edges. */\ntype HarnessEdgeRunner = Readonly<{\n  runFork<A, E>(effect: Effect.Effect<A, E>): Fiber.Fiber<A, E>;\n  runPromiseExit<A, E>(effect: Effect.Effect<A, E>): Promise<Exit.Exit<A, E>>;\n}>;\n\ntype HarnessRuntimeOptions = Readonly<{\n  projectListing?: Readonly<{\n    list(): Promise<ProjectListResult>;\n    stop: Effect.Effect<void, unknown>;\n  }>;\n  /** Runs the shutdown program and the registration edge; the global runtime by default. */\n  runtime?: HarnessEdgeRunner;\n  projectRegistration?: ProjectRegistrationFlow;\n  // The harness's shared application database authority, stopped last when Storage stopped.\n  applicationDatabase?: Readonly<{ stop(): Promise<void>; withhold(): void }>;\n  transport: HarnessTransport;\n  canonicalProjectApplication: CanonicalProjectApplication;\n  projectStorageApplication: ProjectStorageApplication;\n  workspaceApplication: WorkspaceApplication;\n  harnessVersion: string;\n  createId: () => string;\n  now: () => string;\n}>;\n\nconst failureMessages: Readonly<\n  Record<\n    Extract<HarnessFailureCode, \"PROTOCOL_MESSAGE_INVALID\" | \"PROTOCOL_VERSION_UNSUPPORTED\">,\n    string\n  >\n> = {\n  PROTOCOL_MESSAGE_INVALID: harnessFailureMessages.PROTOCOL_MESSAGE_INVALID,\n  PROTOCOL_VERSION_UNSUPPORTED: harnessFailureMessages.PROTOCOL_VERSION_UNSUPPORTED,\n};\n\nfunction runtimeShutdownFailure(failures: readonly unknown[]): unknown {\n  return failures.length === 1\n    ? failures[0]\n    : new AggregateError(failures, \"Harness runtime shutdown failed.\");\n}\n\ntype StopFailures = readonly unknown[];\n\n// Invokes a cleanup in the caller's turn (same-turn admission is part of the shutdown\n// contract) and exposes its settlement as an Effect that never fails. The settlement is\n// attached in that turn too, so a failed cleanup whose program never runs is still handled.\nfunction startStop(stop: () => Promise<void> | undefined): Effect.Effect<StopFailures> {\n  let started: Promise<void> | undefined;\n  try {\n    started = stop();\n  } catch (error) {\n    return Effect.succeed([error]);\n  }\n  const settled = Promise.resolve(started).then(\n    (): StopFailures => [],\n    (error: unknown) => [error],\n  );\n  return Effect.promise(() => settled);\n}\n\n/**\n * Forks an Effect cleanup on `runtime` in the caller's turn and exposes its settlement as an\n * Effect: a failure or defect becomes a reported failure, and an interruption interrupts the\n * caller, so an interrupted cleanup is never reported as clean.\n */\nfunction startEffectStop(\n  runtime: HarnessEdgeRunner,\n  stop: Effect.Effect<void, unknown> | undefined,\n): Effect.Effect<StopFailures> {\n  if (stop === undefined) return Effect.succeed([]);\n  const fiber = runtime.runFork(stop);\n  return Effect.flatMap(Fiber.await(fiber), (exit) => {\n    if (Exit.isSuccess(exit)) return Effect.succeed([]);\n    if (Cause.hasInterruptsOnly(exit.cause)) return Effect.interrupt;\n    return Effect.succeed([Cause.squash(exit.cause)]);\n  });\n}\n\n// While a Project is held, Storage stops only after canonical release succeeds (a failed\n// release may still hold it). While none is held, Storage stops together with the canonical\n// application, so a running upgrade meets the owner stop instead of finishing first. The\n// shared application database stops only after Storage stopped and the listing drained, so a\n// withheld Storage keeps it alive.\ntype StorageStopOutcome = Readonly<{ withheld: boolean; failures: StopFailures }>;\n\n/** Whether a Project is held; a query that throws counts as held and is reported. */\nfunction heldProject(\n  options: HarnessRuntimeOptions,\n): Readonly<{ held: boolean; failures: StopFailures }> {\n  try {\n    return { held: options.canonicalProjectApplication.holdsProject?.() ?? true, failures: [] };\n  } catch (error) {\n    return { held: true, failures: [error] };\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Stops the canonical application and Storage in the order the held Project allows. With no\n * Project held, Storage stops at once and its own result alone decides whether the shared\n * application database may stop; a canonical failure is still reported.\n */\nfunction stopCanonicalThenStorage(\n  options: HarnessRuntimeOptions,\n): Effect.Effect<StorageStopOutcome> {\n  const query = heldProject(options);\n  const canonicalStop = startStop(() => options.canonicalProjectApplication.stop());\n  const canonical = Effect.map(canonicalStop, (failures) => [...query.failures, ...failures]);\n  const storage = Effect.map(\n    Effect.suspend(() => startStop(() => options.projectStorageApplication.stop())),\n    (failures) => ({ withheld: failures.length > 0, failures }),\n  );\n  if (query.held) {\n    return Effect.flatMap(canonicalStop, (failures) =>\n      failures.length > 0\n        ? Effect.succeed({ withheld: true, failures: [...query.failures, ...failures] })\n        : Effect.map(storage, (stopped) => ({\n            withheld: stopped.withheld,\n            failures: [...query.failures, ...stopped.failures],\n          })),\n    );\n  }\n  return Effect.map(\n    Effect.all([canonical, storage], { concurrency: \"unbounded\" }),\n    ([canonicalFailures, stopped]) => ({\n      withheld: stopped.withheld,\n      failures: [...canonicalFailures, ...stopped.failures],\n    }),\n  );\n}\n\nfunction shutdownAfterIntake(\n  options: HarnessRuntimeOptions,\n  runtime: HarnessEdgeRunner,\n  intakeFailures: StopFailures,\n): Effect.Effect<void, unknown> {\n  const storage = stopCanonicalThenStorage(options);\n  const listing = startEffectStop(runtime, options.projectListing?.stop);\n  return Effect.gen(function* () {\n    const [storageOutcome, listingFailures] = yield* Effect.all([storage, listing], {\n      concurrency: \"unbounded\",\n    });\n    // A withheld Storage keeps the shared database alive; it is told so and never stopped.\n    if (storageOutcome.withheld) options.applicationDatabase?.withhold();\n    const databaseFailures = storageOutcome.withheld\n      ? []\n      : yield* Effect.suspend(() => startStop(() => options.applicationDatabase?.stop()));\n    const failures = [\n      ...intakeFailures,\n      ...storageOutcome.failures,\n      ...listingFailures,\n      ...databaseFailures,\n    ];\n    if (failures.length > 0) return yield* Effect.fail(runtimeShutdownFailure(failures));\n  });\n}\n\ntype CanonicalProjectMessage = Extract<\n  DesktopMessage,\n  { command: \"project.activate\" | \"project.switch\" | \"project.command\" | \"project.upgrade\" }\n>;\ntype ProjectStorageMessage = Extract<\n  DesktopMessage,\n  { command: \"project.open\" | \"project.create\" | \"project.close\" }\n>;\ntype WorkspaceMessage = Extract<\n  DesktopMessage,\n  { command: \"workspace.query\" | \"workspace.intent\" }\n>;\ntype RegistryMessage = Extract<\n  DesktopMessage,\n  { command: \"project.list\" | \"project.registration\" }\n>;\ntype ConversationMessage = Extract<\n  DesktopMessage,\n  { command: \"project.conversation.read\" | \"project.conversation.save\" }\n>;\nfunction isConversationMessage(message: DesktopMessage): message is ConversationMessage {\n  return (\n    message.command === \"project.conversation.read\" ||\n    message.command === \"project.conversation.save\"\n  );\n}\nfunction isWorkspaceMessage(message: DesktopMessage): message is WorkspaceMessage {\n  return message.command === \"workspace.query\" || message.command === \"workspace.intent\";\n}\nfunction isCanonicalProjectMessage(message: DesktopMessage): message is CanonicalProjectMessage {\n  return (\n    message.command === \"project.activate\" ||\n    message.command === \"project.switch\" ||\n    message.command === \"project.command\" ||\n    message.command === \"project.upgrade\"\n  );\n}\nfunction isProjectStorageMessage(message: DesktopMessage): message is ProjectStorageMessage {\n  return [\"project.open\", \"project.create\", \"project.close\"].includes(message.command);\n}\n\nexport function startHarnessRuntime(options: HarnessRuntimeOptions): StopHarnessRuntime {\n  let sequence = 0;\n  const runtime: HarnessEdgeRunner = options.runtime ?? {\n    runFork: Effect.runFork,\n    runPromiseExit: Effect.runPromiseExit,\n  };\n  // Set once stop begins; an interruption after that is the dispose ending a request.\n  let stopPromise: Promise<void> | undefined;\n\n  const nextMetadata = <CausationId extends MessageId | null>(causationId: CausationId) => {\n    sequence += 1;\n    return {\n      messageId: options.createId(),\n      sentAt: options.now(),\n      sequence,\n      causationId,\n    };\n  };\n\n  const sendFailure = (\n    causationId: MessageId | null,\n    failure: Readonly<{ code: HarnessFailureCode; message: string; retryable: boolean }>,\n  ): void => {\n    options.transport.send(\n      causationId === null\n        ? createSystemFailureEvent(nextMetadata(null), failure)\n        : createRequestFailureEvent(nextMetadata(causationId), failure),\n    );\n  };\n\n  const sendInternalFailure = (causationId: MessageId | null): void => {\n    sendFailure(causationId, {\n      code: \"HARNESS_INTERNAL_FAILURE\",\n      message: harnessFailureMessages.HARNESS_INTERNAL_FAILURE,\n      retryable: false,\n    });\n  };\n\n  const handleCanonicalProjectMessage = async (message: CanonicalProjectMessage): Promise<void> => {\n    if (message.command === \"project.activate\") {\n      const result = await options.canonicalProjectApplication.activate(message.payload);\n      options.transport.send(\n        createProjectActivateResultEvent(nextMetadata(message.messageId), result),\n      );\n    } else if (message.command === \"project.upgrade\") {\n      const result = await options.canonicalProjectApplication.upgrade(message.payload);\n      options.transport.send(\n        createProjectUpgradeResultEvent(nextMetadata(message.messageId), result),\n      );\n    } createProjectSwitchResultEvent(nextMetadata(message.messageId), result),\n      );\n    } else {\n      const result = await options.canonicalProjectApplication.execute(message.payload);\n      options.transport.send(\n        createProjectCommandResultEvent(nextMetadata(message.messageId), result),\n      );\n    }\n  };\n\n  const handleProjectStorageMessage = async (message: );\n        return;\n      }\n      case \"project.close\": {\n        const result = await options.projectStorageApplication.close(message.payload);\n        options.transport.send(\n          createProjectCloseResultEvent(nextMetadata(message.messageId), result),\n        );\n      }\n    }\n  };\n\n  // The Project list and registration steps both answer from the registration registry.\n  const handleRegistryMessage = async (message: RegistryMessage): Promise<void> => {\n    if (message.command === \"project.list\") {\n      const result =\n        (await options.projectListing?.list()) ??\n        ({ status: \"unavailable\", code: \"PROJECT_LIST_UNAVAILABLE\" } as const);\n      options.transport.send(createProjectListResultEvent(nextMetadata(message.messageId), result));\n      return;\n    }\n    const sendResult = (result: Parameters<typeof createProjectRegistrationResultEvent>[1]) =>\n      options.transport.send(\n        createProjectRegistrationResultEvent(nextMetadata(message.messageId), result),\n      );\n    if (options.projectRegistration === undefined) {\n      sendResult({ status: \"unavailable\", code: \"PROJECT_REGISTRATION_UNAVAILABLE\" });\n      return;\n    }\n    const exit = await runtime.runPromiseExit(options.projectRegistration.handle(message.payload));\n    if (Exit.isSuccess(exit)) {\n      sendResult(exit.value);\n      return;\n    }\n    // Once stop began, an interruption is the dispose ending this request: it gets no reply.\n    if (stopPromise !== undefined && Cause.hasInterruptsOnly(exit.cause)) return;\n    sendInternalFailure(message.messageId);\n  };\n\n  const handleConversationMessage = async (message: ConversationMessage): Promise<void> => {\n    if (message.command === \"project.conversation.read\") {\n      const result = await options.canonicalProjectApplication.conversationRead(message.payload);\n      options.transport.send(\n        createProjectConversationReadResultEvent(nextMetadata(message.messageId), result),\n      );\n      return;\n    }\n    const result = await options.canonicalProjectApplication.conversationSave(message.payload);\n    options.transport.send(\n      createProjectConversationSaveResultEvent(nextMetadata(message.messageId), result),\n    );\n  };\n\n  const handleWorkspaceMessage = async (message: WorkspaceMessage): Promise<void> => {\n    // Sequence numbers follow emission order, so metadata is taken after the answer settles.\n    if (message.command === \"workspace.query\") {\n      const result = await options.workspaceApplication.query(message.payload);\n      options.transport.send(\n        createWorkspaceQueryResultEvent(nextMetadata(message.messageId), result),\n      );\n      return;\n    }\n };\n\n  const handleMessage = async (message: unknown): Promise<void> => {\n    const parsed = parseDesktopMessage(message);\n    const causationId = readMessageId(message);\n\n    if (!parsed.ok) {\n      sendFailure(causationId, {\n        code: parsed.error.code,\n        message: failureMessages[parsed.error.code],\n        retryable: false,\n      });\n      return;\n    }\n\n    if (isCanonicalProjectMessage(parsed.value)) return handleCanonicalProjectMessage(parsed.value);\n    if (isProjectStorageMessage(parsed.value)) return handleProjectStorageMessage(parsed.value);\n    if (isWorkspaceMessage(parsed.value)) return handleWorkspaceMessage(parsed.value);\n    if (isConversationMessage(parsed.value)) return handleConversationMessage(parsed.value);\n    if (parsed.value.command === \"system.handshake\") {\n      options.transport.send(createReadyEvent(nextMetadata(causationId), options.harnessVersion));\n      return;\n    }\n    return handleRegistryMessage(parsed.value);\n  };\n\n  const stopMessages = options.transport.subscribe((message) => {\n    void handleMessage(message).catch(() => {\n      sendInternalFailure(readMessageId(message));\n    });\n  });\n  const stopNotifications = options.workspaceApplication.subscribe((notification) => {\n    try {\n      const validated = decodeStrict(WorkspaceNotificationSchema, notification);\n      options.transport.send(\n        createWorkspaceProjectionInvalidatedEvent(nextMetadata(null), validated),\n      );\n    } catch {\n      sendInternalFailure(null);\n    }\n  });\n\n  // One completion shared by every stop request, published before any cleanup callback\n  // runs so a callback that re-enters stop receives the same promise.\n  const completion = Deferred.makeUnsafe<void, unknown>();\n  return () => {\n    if (stopPromise !== undefined) {\n      return stopPromise;\n    }\n    stopPromise = Effect.runPromise(Deferred.await(completion));\n    const intakeFailures: unknown[] = [];\n    try {\n      stopMessages();\n    } catch (error) {\n      intakeFailures.push(error);\n    }\n    try {\n      stopNotifications();\n    } catch (error) {\n      intakeFailures.push(error);\n    }\n    // The program's exit is observed on the global runtime, so the stop settles even when a\n    // dispose interrupts the program, or when a disposed runtime never runs it.\n    const program = runtime.runFork(shutdownAfterIntake(options, runtime, intakeFailures));\n    Effect.runFork(Effect.flatMap(Fiber.await(program), (exit) => Deferred.done(completion, exit)));\n    return stopPromise;\n  };\n}\n"},"apps/harness/src/process-bootstrap.ts":{"language":"typescript","mutants":[{"id":"66","mutatorName":"StringLiteral","replacement":"\"\"","statusReason":"Static mutant (and \"ignoreStatic\" error.code === \"ENOENT\";\n}\n\nfunction parentOf(root: string): string {\n  const parent = path.dirname(root);\n  if (parent === root) throw new Error(\"Harness bootstrap root has no existing ancestor.\");\n  return parent;\n}\n\nfunction physicalRoot(root: string): string {\n  const missingSegments: string[] = [];\n  let cursor = root;\n  for (;;) {\n    try {\n      const entry = lstatSync(cursor);\n      if (entry.isSymbolicLink()) {\n        throw new Error(\"Harness bootstrap root must not be a symbolic link.\");\n      }\n      return path.join(realpathSync.native(cursor), ...missingSegments.reverse());\n    } catch (error: unknown) {\n      if (!isMissingPathError(error)) throw error;\n      missingSegments.push(path.basename(cursor));\n      cursor = parentOf(cursor);\n    }\n  }\n}\n\nfunction trustedRoot(value: string): string {\n  const root = path.resolve(fileURLToPath(value));\n  if (!path.isAbsolute(root)) {\n    throw new Error(\"Harness bootstrap root must resolve absolutely.\");\n  }\n  return physicalRoot(root);\n}\n\nfunction rootsOverlap(first: string, second: string): boolean {\n  const relative = path.relative(first, second);\n failed.\";\n\n/** One failure is reported as is; two are reported together, in order, never replaced. */\nfunction combinedFailure(failures: readonly unknown[], message: CombinedFailureMessage): unknown {\n  return failures.length === 1 ? failures[0] : new AggregateError(failures, message);\n}\n\n/**\n * Disposes `runtime` and answers its failures, at most one. The dispose closes its scope\n * uninterruptibly, so it runs detached and only the wait for it is bounded: past the deadline\n * it keeps running while the process exits.\n */\nfunction boundedDispose(\n  runtime: Pick<ManagedRuntime.ManagedRuntime<unknown, unknown>, \"disposeEffect\">,\n  deadlineMs: number,\n): Effect.Effect<readonly unknown[]> {\n  return Effect.forkDetach(runtime.disposeEffect).pipe(\n    Effect.flatMap(Fiber.await),\n    Effect.map((exit) => (Exit.isSuccess(exit) ? \"./observer-limits.js\";\n\ntype Closed = Readonly<{ status: string }>;\n\n/** What the Project listing adapter owns: the registration owner, observer and registry. */\nexport type RegistrationListingPorts = Readonly<{\n  owner: Readonly<{ listProjects(): Promise<unknown>; close(): Promise<Closed> }>;\n  observer: Readonly<{ closeEffect: Effect.Effect<Closed> }>;\n  registry: Readonly<{ stop(): Promise<void> }>;\n}>;\n\n/** A registration part whose cleanup at stop was not confirmed. */\ntype CleanupPart = \"registry\" | \"observer\" | \"listing\";\n\n/** Stop could not confirm every part's cleanup; each unconfirmed part is named. */\nclass RegistrationCleanupUnconfirmed extends Data.TaggedError(\"RegistrationCleanupUnconfirmed\")<{\n  readonly message: string;\n  readonly parts: readonly CleanupPart[];\n  readonly cause?: unknown;\n}> {}\n\n/** The failure naming `parts`; it carries a cause only when there is one. */\nfunction cleanupUnconfirmed(parts: readonly CleanupPart[], cause: unknown) {\n  const message = `Project registration cleanup is unconfirmed: ${parts.join(\", \")}.`;\n  return new RegistrationCleanupUnconfirmed(\n    cause === undefined ? { message, parts } : { message, parts, cause },\n  );\n}\n\n/** What the cleanup chain has seen so far; an answer not yet seen counts as unconfirmed. */\ntype CleanupProgress = { listing?: Closed; observer?: Closed };\n\nfunction unconfirmedParts(progress: CleanupProgress, registryUnconfirmed: boolean) {\n  const parts: CleanupPart[] = [];\n  if (registryUnconfirmed) parts;\n}\n\n/**\n * Closes the owner and the observer, then stops the registry they share, all within one\n * shutdown budget. Out of budget, the part in progress is abandoned, no later part starts,\n * and every part not confirmed by then is named; a failed registry stop is the cause.\n */\nfunction stopWithinBudget(registration: RegistrationListingPorts) {\n  return Effect.suspend(() => {\n    const progress: CleanupProgress = {};\n    const chain = Effect.gen(function* () {\n      progress.listing = yield* Effect.promise(() => registration.owner.close());\n      progress.observer = yield* registration.observer.closeEffect;\n      const registry = yield* Effect.result(\n        Effect.tryPromise({ try: () => registration.registry.stop(), catch: (cause) => cause }),\n      );\n      return Result.isFailure(registry)\n        ? { parts: unconfirmedParts(progress, true), cause: registry.failure }\n        : { parts: unconfirmedParts(progress, false), cause: undefined };\n    });\n    return chain.pipe(\n      Effect.timeoutOrElse({\n        duration: REGISTRATION_SHUTDOWN_CLEANUP_BUDGET_MS,\n        orElse: () => Effect.succeed({ parts: unconfirmedParts(progress, true), cause: undefined }),\n      }),\n      Effect.flatMap(({ parts, cause }) =>\n        parts.length === 0 ? Effect.void : Effect.fail(cleanupUnconfirmed(parts, cause)),\n      ),\n    );\n  });\n}\n\n// Listing and registration share the registry: it stops after the owner and the observer.\nexport function createRegistrationListing(registration: RegistrationListingPorts) {\n  return {\n    list: async () =>\n      decodeStrict(ProjectListResultSchema, await registration.owner.listProjects()),\n    stop: Deferred.succeed(closeOutcome, Exit.isSuccess(exit) ? exit.value : cleanupUnconfirmed),\n        ),\n      ),\n    );\n  });\n  return {\n    closeEffect,\n    close: () => is held\", () => {\n  const canonicalFailure = new Error(\"canonical stop failed\");\n  const storageFailure = new Error(\"storage stop failed\");\n  const listingFailure = new Error(\"listing stop {\n    canonical: canonicalFailure,\n    storage: storageFailure,\n    listing: listingFailure,\n  } as const;\n  type Failing = keyof typeof failureOf;\n\n  /** A runtime whose owners named in   stop: Effect.fail(listingFailure),\n    };\n    const f = canonicalRuntimeFixture(\n      () => false,\n      failing.includes(\"listing\") ? listing : undefined,\n    );\n    if f;\n  }\n\n  /** One failure is reported as is; several are reported as one aggregate, in order. */\n  function expectShutdownError(error: unknown, failures: readonly Error[]) {\n    if (failures.length === 1) expect(error).toBe(failures[0]);\n    else {\n      expect(error).toBeInstanceOf(AggregateError);\n      expect((error as AggregateError).errors).toEqual(failures);\n    }\n  }\n\n  it.for<{ name: string; failing: readonly Failing[]; databaseStops: boolean }>([\n    { name: \"canonical fails\", failing: [\"canonical\"], databaseStops: true },\n    { name: \"Storage [\"listing\"], databaseStops: true },\n  ])(\"$name\", async (row) => {\n    const f = failingRuntime(row.failing);\n    const error = await f.stop().catch((caught: unknown) => caught);\n {\n    promise,\n    resolve(value: T): void {\n      if (settle === undefined) {\n        throw new Error(\"Deferred promise was not initialized.\");\n      }\n      settle(value);\n    },\n  };\n}\n\nasync function unexpectedCanonicalSwitch(): Promise<never> {\n  throw new Error(\"Unexpected canonical Project switch in this fixture.\");\n}\n\nfunction unusedCanonicalApplication() {\n
